# Re:Zero — master longitudinal ledger

## 0. Authority, history, and use

This is the canonical cumulative state ledger for the Re:Zero Japanese main-light-novel analysis. It is mutable current authority through the latest verified volume transaction. Frozen volume readings remain the authority for what was supportable at each historical prospective horizon and for their detailed source-facing arguments.

This ledger was created after V02 during the project-initiation architecture repair. The V01/V02 state below is a controlled backfill from the already frozen canonical readings; the ledger did not govern those readings prospectively and neither freeze was modified. Exact source evidence was consulted only where necessary to validate the source identities already recorded by those artifacts.

For every new volume, update this file only after the source-facing reading has identified the material transitions. Preserve revision ancestry and route to the relevant VNN section or exact EPUB locus. Do not duplicate an independently promoted ledger here beyond a routing/current-summary row.

| Minerva, reader-facing aim | Wants ordinary lives with family/lovers without tears after escalating force fails. | Peace/ordinary happiness orient the motive, not only spectacular healing. | No complete cost theory or shown current cast receipt.; part0016#B0001–0058 |

Earth parents receive no current message. | part0010#B0003–0555 / part0017#B0101–0189 |

The false statement supplies no reliable clan custom. | part0018#B0220–0270 |

The repeated learned habits and particular comfort have reconstruction responsibilities. They distinguish stable care, practiced skill, a chosen self-presentation and a specific way of joking from effects created only by immediate danger. Unknown favorite foods, unobserved private routines and future family scenes remain absent.

| Theresia’s repeated questions | Flower preference, sword purpose and wanted verbal love recur with different relational stakes. | Track actual speaker and recipient; a repeated formula is not an identical answer every time. B19–21, p-008 B0060–0568. |

This prospective body follows separate acceptance of repaired V24 saved content, source correspondence, name references and fidelity at 2026-11-07T01:38:34.878760Z. Each operation must close its complete gate before the next. CI owns integration. Keep this body literal when those statuses change, with an entering overlay for newly verified gates.

The source-facing high-water is Japanese main LN V24, with 672 propositions. The 605 predecessor decisions are 366 PRESERVE, 178 STRENGTHEN, 53 REVISE and eight OPEN, with 67 new propositions. The five owners carry E030, K987, M626, I521, R207 and RT600. Each of 197 entering current relationships received a disposition and 548 earlier dated transitions were individually checked at exact identity, using their accepted historical semantics. The verified integrated sequential high-water remains V23 until V24's own gate closes. V23, Shaula V21–V23 and Patrasche V01–V23 closed their gates before V24. Keep the 24 entering model, default and review scopes and 75 protected artifacts exact. No prior grade automatically includes V24 or V25.

The census is 30 confirmed Subaru deaths and 30 arrived segments. V24 retrospectively confirms E027 death 27 and shows E028 Green Room waking. E028 ends in fatal shadow collapse. E029 starts in Taygeta immediately after the Corridor encounter, uses a shortened window, and ends when Reid's sword strike kills Subaru amid the shadow. E030 is shown later at the balcony, with the actual waking moment omitted. Subaru jumps deliberately while human Shaula tries to catch him. She changes and aims in the fall. Impact kills him before the tail fires. No E031 is depicted. The observable end of each person's state, any subsequently reported outcome and a verified next arrival retain separate records.

Louis admits taking Subaru's earlier local memories and describes a somewhat different initial entry to the Corridor. The murder controller, initial selected book or ingress, earlier inscriptions, corpse mover and balcony decapitator remain open. Subaru claims continuity after the Rem-shaped encouragement and keeps his post-loss experience without demonstrated old-memory restoration. In E028 Lye takes Emilia's name from others' recognition while Subaru remembers her. E029 reaches Ram earlier and preserves her recognition of Emilia. Lye dies in E029 and Ram explicitly still cannot recall Rem. The other local observations do not establish a permanent or universal failure of restoration. Physical Rem remains asleep. Human Anastasia has no new awake speech or assent. The artificial operator acts and accepts custody of Rem and Patrasche. Julius reports Roy's body under Reid's control in E029. Exact future outcome, original body, chronology and all transfer rules remain open.

Cor Leonis is named at the Greed factor awakening and has an observed approximate location and condition effect. Other use, range and precise cause remain to be tested. Shaula's form continuity is now positive. She reports an unspecified rule breach, asks for an order to kill herself, reveals that destruction of the Trial is permitted, and tries to save Subaru before her converted body aims. Her earlier selective warmth and cruelty, the stated preference to preserve their time, actual chosen withholding, and involuntary harm are separate. Rule four prohibits destroying the tower itself. The exact trigger, actor, detection channel and command limit remain open. No group pass, retrieved universal remedy, fully negotiated romance or completed rescue is established. Lye's local death and E029's recognitions do not automatically carry into E030 or a later branch.
